# exp12_transfer

E12 threshold transfer. Each rule keeps the alarm threshold tuned in exp10 without attack (paper Table IV: unfiltered 0.75, majority 0.85, coordinate median 0.85, W-MSR F=2 0.35, FTM 0.90, oracle 0.65; ByzantineFlood and the single camera use their normal output) and is tested in seven settings, without attack and under AW with 2 Byzantine cameras. The full tau sweep is run in every setting, so the threshold can also be re-tuned there (scripts/make_figures.py, TRANSFER.md, tab_transfer).

Detector: {'type': 'belief_bank', 'bank': 'detector_base'}  
Seeds: 50, rounds: 20, runtime: 7.3 min
